## 2026-10-17T23:39:48.943Z — DatasetStore equal-time segment comparator overflow reproduction requested
- Complete current `DatasetStore.java` review found a bounded ordering hazard: equal-created-time segments are compared with subtraction, which overflows for extreme integer segment values and can select an older ended segment as newest.
- Added one public-load regression using `Integer.MIN_VALUE` ended and `Integer.MAX_VALUE` open segments for the same run and timestamp. The correct result is one unfinished logical run with both level entries.
- This commit is reproduction-only. Production code, raw histories, saves, scores, gameplay, APK and executable build request remain unchanged. Core-only CI must retain the focused failure before correction. TEST275 remains the latest fully gated private candidate; TEST262 remains the latest owner publication.

## Scroll gestures beginning on buttons and links, pending candidate (2026-10-17)
The owner reports that swiping a page does nothing when the touch begins on a button. Confirmed source mechanism: child controls register after the ScrollPane and consume DOWN, so the scroll listener never captures the drag. Bounded opt-in `ScrollPane.allowDragFromButtons()` reorders the pane listener ahead of child controls but passes the initial touch through. Once movement crosses the pre-existing threshold, it flags that pointer as a scroll, causing `PointerArea` to release both buttons and links without firing a click; a stationary tap still works. Fast UP-only swipes are also handled. Enabled on APD Settings, About, and Cleric Grudge; other ScrollPanes (including UIMSD row pickers and Custom Rules direction-locked sliders) are deliberately unchanged. Retained `ScrollClickSuppressionTest` for swipe/tap/cancel identity and extended the existing API-34 Adult humor touch test to swipe from a checkbox without toggling before tapping it normally. **No test evidence yet; no APK or distribution**. Source candidate should go through core-only gate first (1776 methods), then full existing 38-method native/history/debug/visual verification on intentionally new APK if core succeeds. TEST268 staged and TEST262 published remain fallbacks. Detailed notes: `docs/APD-BUTTON-ORIGIN-SCROLL-2026-10-17.md`.

## TEST266 UIMSD hidden-question consistency accepted; staging profile corrected — 2026-10-17T07:08:00Z
Current `Question.java` and `Questions.java` review with `WndUimsdAsk` confirmed a bounded live mismatch: after selecting internal question parts, turning Hidden details off left those parts in the displayed sentence although QueryEngine rejected them. ## Supported UIMSD Markdown export defect — 2026-10-16T21:58:10Z

Separate from the GenerationEvents/GameFacts review below, ReportRenderer.escapeMarkdownCell currently escapes pipes before existing backslashes. A supported value containing backslash-plus-pipe (a\|b) can render with an unintended Markdown table delimiter after the slash pair is consumed. Raw history, queries, caches and scores are unaffected. The bounded display-only correction is to double existing backslashes before escaping pipes, retain newline-to-space behavior, and add a permanent one-cell structural regression. This correction is not in TEST258 and has not yet landed or passed tests. Audit: docs/APD-UIMSD-MARKDOWN-ESCAPE-AUDIT-2026-10-16.md.

## UIMSD raw-preview follow-up — 2026-10-10

Source tracing found that ordinary text wrapping never splits an overlong token. Raw queued JSON/IDs can therefore still overflow despite a max-width setting, and the default text renderer treats underscores/double stars as highlighting markers rather than literal payload characters.

Status/queue messages now use a raw variant of the existing scrolling UIMSD window. It preserves literal markers, breaks overlong tokens using the actual font width, keeps Unicode code points intact, and restores its raw/structured mode alongside the exact original payload. Presentation-only newlines do not change queued, exported or stored data. Structured report/help windows retain their existing formatting.

Local evidence: changed production classes compile; seven permanent wrapping tests cover unchanged normal whitespace, long IDs, proportional widths, Unicode, literal markers, oversized single glyphs and a 100,000-character input. The initial test compilation failed because the regex escape was generated incorrectly; it was corrected before the passing run. Native preview assertions now include a long ID and visibly rendered literal markers at narrow width, across two scene resets.

Earlier dialog-only source 68ae1fa35c56c7cc0361ee5cd0118ddb93d9127d completed the full core/build and API 34 workflow 38091430139 successfully. Its artifacts are staging evidence, not a delivered/signing-checked replacement for TEST26. Complete/native verification of the combined saved-view, Community and raw-preview changes is requested separately. Phone acceptance and process-death restoration remain unverified.

## 2026-10-10 22:31 UTC — Saved-view corruption protection

New deterministic regressions confirmed a saved-view data-loss bug: load skipped damaged/duplicate rows, then a normal save, rename or delete replaced the original file with only the readable subset. Invalid UTF-8 hidden inside valid Base64 was also silently converted into replacement characters.

Fix: reject replacement after any incomplete load before creating a temporary file; decode Base64 payloads with strict UTF-8. Valid views remain readable and UIMSD explains why edits are paused rather than reporting a corrupt file as an empty history. Unsupported but structurally valid future queries are retained byte-for-byte and do not block saving other views.

Evidence: regression failed on baseline for malformed replacement; separate invalid-UTF-8 case failed before strict decoding. Final local targeted suite: 39 tests passed (saved views/model/combined scope/settings). Initial broad invocation named nonexistent SavedQuestionsTest and executed no substantive checks; corrected invocation is the evidence, not that failed attempt. No native/complete build evidence yet for this new fix. TEST27 verification of the earlier dialog fix is independent and pending.

## Pending UIMSD reliability audit — C26–C29

Owner prioritizes UIMSD and forbids game-breaking crashes/freezes/save loss/gameplay interference. This audit does not promise zero bugs. C26: mixed-rules exclusion counters count table rows, but status text labeled them runs (five items from one run became five runs); use actual table units and singular/plural, without changing history/count policy. Two reproduction tests failed before and pass after.

C27: OutboxStore intentionally skips/counts damaged lines, but queue mutations ignored that result and rewrote the file, permanently removing unreadable entries. Reject updates and sending from a partial read; preserve original queue bytes. All mutating loads and pre/post-send loads use the guard. Three corruption cases failed before/pass after: add, remove and initial send. A fourth regression reproduces corruption during a fake gateway acknowledgement: retain the damaged file, report the real acknowledgement and record it rather than pretending nothing was sent. No real server enabled or contacted by these tests; offline operations remain local. A retained acknowledged entry may need an idempotent retry; no automatic deletion/repair is introduced.

C28: PrivacySummary treated unreadable queues as empty and all envelopes as finished-run summaries (even settings/evidence). It now warns when count is incomplete/unknown and describes queued items accurately, including explicit-consent full-log evidence. Three new controlled cases failed before/pass after; normal count wording expectation updated. It never claims a transmission from queueing/inspection. C29: Home now explicitly explains that unknown/changed-rule runs and repeated imported copies are excluded, with table Options to include them; no unlock/count policy changed or history removed.

Audit coverage: 65 targeted engine/saved-view/column/queue/archive/privacy-safety checks passed before the queue hardening. New independent combined-query oracle passes192 scope/filter/sort/column arrangements after actual saved-view disk reloads, checking expected row identity/order, equal-value stability and displayed column order. Hidden-information safety compares24,024 query outcomes across histories whose hidden facts differ; no observable differences. Queue/status/combined/safety batch28checks pass2.199s. Existing+new local555checks pass17.399s before final Home clarification; targeted Home/status four checks pass0.150s afterward. Complete core/native/build acceptance still pending for these changes. CI will also exercise recorder failure containment, recording-on/off generation/combat/item safety and unchanged golden baselines. These comparisons are test loops, not thousands of separately reported unit tests.

Remaining: physical RedMagic app-switch/touch/layout/performance checks, Android process-death restoration of unsupported dialog types, real older-APK upgrades/exact owner saves, full normal-run quest/boss walkthroughs, and the owner's exact archive. ## C20 — Export must not claim success for an incomplete manifest

Controlled reproduction: a finished temporary log changes after manifest planning but before the second read (output-stream hook, no timing sleeps). Old exporter silently skipped the changed log, finished a ZIP still listing that missing entry, and reported success. Regression16 tests /1failure before. The importer safely rejects such an archive; no history loss was found.

Minimal production repair: detect missing/changed second-read content and return failed export with an explicit incomplete-archive/retry explanation instead of success. Existing originals, unfinished-run redaction and immutable redacted snapshots are unchanged; no extra whole-history memory buffering. The test mutates only its temporary fixture. Targeted16pass0.476s and broader533pass18.605s after repair.

Potential effect: exports in this exceptional change-during-read condition now fail instead of claiming partial success. A partial ZIP can remain at the chosen output location; do not use it as a backup, export again. Completed/unfinished ordinary round trips, archive imports, no-overwrite and hidden-information regressions pass. Physical-phone verification and the next full Android build for this new archive source remain pending.

C18 Android verification is running from source65403163ebecbf27f36817f6882bf91cbf9bfa61 / run37966964739. That staged candidate does not include this later C20 repair. TEST10 remains the owner's current playable phone-test APK; do not mislabel staged or local code as delivered.

## Resume protocol (owner's standing rules)
- After each completed step (tests pass, committed, pushed, this file updated): cancel the earlier resume and schedule ONE new one-shot `send_later` about 5 h 10 min ahead. Message: "Resume: read docs/HANDOFF.md, continue from the next task, don't redo finished work, don't ask me for decisions already recorded." When the queue is finished, cancel it and schedule nothing.
- If a usage-limit message shows a reset time, record it here and schedule the resume for that time + 10 min (weekly limit: same with the weekly reset). Never retry requests while limited.
- When a resume fires: at most 3 attempts without a new commit. Still blocked: stop, record why here, do not schedule a fourth.
- Unattended: no product decisions (record them under Open questions and move to a task that does not need them). Never force-push, change signing keys, or touch anything outside the current task. Never lower testing or code quality to save usage. If a step cannot be finished properly, stop at a clean commit.

## Further controlled UIMSD corruption regressions
Three additional confirmed robustness bugs investigated against separately compiled canonical source:
1. RunIndex unsigned64 count overflows Java signedlong, bypasses iteration and accepts empty derived index with valid CRC instead of rebuilding. One-line count bounds restore existing logs without modifying them.
2. RunLogReader unsigned64 frame length overflows/casts to zero, accepting malformed unknown frame as valid. One-line nonnegative length guard rejects it.
3. RunLogReader resync's sparse-string hole creation exhausts memory for a tiny valid-checksum frame declaring Integer.MAX_VALUE ID. Reproduced as OutOfMemoryError in isolated -Xmx32m JVM; bound recovery IDs by input file length before allocating holes. No production caller enabling resync found in reviewed snapshot, so this is reader recovery API robustness, not a claimed observed phone crash.
Five new tests across RunIndexCountValidationTest, RunLogFrameLengthValidationTest and RunLogResyncAllocationTest. 42 reader/index/recovery/codec tests passed (including32MiB heap rerun); combined110 archive/reader tests passed. Existing signed64 extreme-value encoding, valid future frames and legitimate empty indexes preserved. Not in delivered TEST45. Full CI for this batch pending; retain prior verified fallback.


## Writer integrity fixes and combined regression evidence
Controlled create race: a path appearing after exists() was truncated by new FileOutputStream(file). Exact canonical regression replaced5 bytes of existing evidence with28 bytes of new header. Atomic createNewFile claim now refuses the competing path with FAILED_EXISTS and preserves bytes. Normal create/append/recovery behavior retained.
Controlled close timeout: queued close returned true before blocked worker wrote data; repeated close also claimed completion. Return success now requires completed close request; repeated calls report pending while worker alive. Gate-released worker still finishes/seals the accepted event normally. No event discard added.
Two new permanent writer tests;37 targeted writer/index/log tests passed. Combined current archive/index/reader/writer regression run:130 tests, zero failures,12.678 seconds. Existing queue overflow, failed disks, multi-thread recording, tail recovery and ordinary log roundtrips passed. No physical-phone verification is claimed.
Previous archive-only source b76bc1cd5e48899b721ca6168cc0bcaabcd3557b/run38143393376 completed core, debug assembly and API34 checks successfully; not privately published. This batch requests full verification of all accumulated fixes. Last delivered verified APK remains TEST45, unchanged. Comprehensive audit still incomplete.


## UIMSD audit continuation — 2026-10-11
Controlled regressions confirmed text grouping delimiter collisions and malformed UTF-8 silently replaced inside Base64 queue fields. QueryEngine now length-prefixes text group components; OutboxStore strictly decodes encoded fields and reports malformed lines without modifying stored bytes. ArchiveImporter builds a first-match-preserving lookup once, avoiding repeated linear manifest scans. New permanent tests cover these cases and valid Unicode/duplicate-path compatibility. Targeted archive/query suite: 90 passed; queue suite: 37 passed. Initial queue run used stale compiled Outbox and failed two existing tests; canonical Outbox recompilation resolved both. ## Grey locked backpack follow-up — Lost Inventory lifecycle checked
Owner reports entire backpack grey/locked, TEST50. Traced UI InventorySlot/InventoryPane/QuickSlotButton LostInventory gating, selector exclusions, null/placeholders and general hero-ready/alive toolbar gating. Capacity never globally greys items. ## TEST88 crystal-path reward correction
# TEST88: restore the approved crystal-path rewards

## Root cause and room identity
The earlier screenshot's six compartments and three crystal keys identify CrystalPathRoom, not CrystalChoiceRoom or CrystalVaultRoom. No new screenshot was attached to the latest report, so the precise floor-22 image cannot independently be matched.

Verified TEST79 source a611c841e64a8bca592831d3200a5a65e1735b61 and current real branch e70f49dbeb3f6fba9becbc015c96027f099a5867 have identical CrystalPathRoom source: original SPD behavior, choosing only one forced Experience/Transmutation reward, allowing exotic substitution, and sorting random rewards into the remaining final compartment. A Retribution scroll in that compartment is permitted by that old code. This is missing implementation on the real branch, not evidence of another placement system overwriting the approved reward.
The stale local checkout contained a proposed both-rewards test, but the actual GitHub branch had no such test. Neither the stale local history nor its uncommitted files were pushed.

## Fix
Reserve one standard Potion of Experience and one standard Scroll of Transmutation before generating the four other items. Reserve their identities for existing duplicate exclusion. Remove these two reserved objects from rarity sorting and place them directly on the two final pedestals. Both final compartments remain behind two crystal doors; three keys, six total rewards, earlier random potion/scroll rooms, geometry, duplicate undoDrop bookkeeping and exploration flags are retained. Standard designated rewards are not replaced by exotic variants. Global Generator probabilities are untouched; necessary room-local random draws differ from the old selection.

Level.drop does not convert or substitute potion/scroll identities. No global drop or exotic system changes were necessary. Existing already-generated floors are not rewritten, and the owner's save/inventory remains untouched. The correction applies to newly generated rooms; it does not replace a Retribution scroll already stored in a save.

## Tests and build status
Untouched baseline failed the repaired fixture: required Transmutation count was zero. An initial headless setup lacked Game.instance and was corrected before the meaningful baseline comparison; that fixture exception was not called a game bug.
Fixed implementation passed one permanent JUnit test with 768 room paints: 64 seeds × six depths (1,6,11,16,22,24) × two exotic settings (0,80). Each checks six total items, exactly one standard Experience and Transmutation, their hidden final-compartment flags, and exactly two crystal-door crossings to each designated prize. The final local run passed in0.853seconds.
Production Java/test compilation passed. Full expected1237core/21API34/18PNG and debug-APK verification are requested for TEST88; pending at this source commit. No TEST88 signed-compatible APK, publication, Android-room screenshot, physical-phone verification or complete normal playthrough is claimed yet.



## Combined TEST88 local verification and deadline
Owner requested all approved backlog by5amEDT October13; not a completion guarantee or demonstrated background worker. Crystal tests expanded to3072paints (four entrances, six depths,64seeds,twoexoticsettings) pass3.562s. UIMSD CellFormatter Math.abs(Long.MIN_VALUE) overflow reproduced; divide/remainder beforeabs preserves formatting across signed boundaries. Two new boundary tests plusformatter/query total26pass0.099s. Combined unique75cache/recovery/storage/query/formatter/crystal tests pass4.512s. No double-counted query cases. Expected full1239core/21API34/18PNG and debug required; pending. Branch update ofprevious eb83commit was rejected byconnector, no actual88APKclaim. Expanded tests andformatter fix are now included in this next verification request; all other backlog stillunfinished.

# TEST90: preserve empty-string saved filters

Source parent 6004e9581222f35290e2da8fb256c9c193b611cc; previous actually produced and delivered TEST89 application source b529832d07a1a2ba30453427edbd346dbf8b4124/run38324730902.

## Confirmed failure
QueryCodec encoded zero text operands and one empty-string operand identically. Decode therefore lost the empty-string operand. Reproduced through the codec API; the player's UI route for constructing such a filter has not been reproduced. This is a saved-query integrity correction, not a claimed player-reported gameplay bug.

## Correction and compatibility
Only the ambiguous single-empty-string filter receives an optional text-count field. Ordinary filters retain their prior encoding. Readers still accept legacy records with no count and retain their existing meaning; old ambiguous records cannot be distinguished retroactively and are not rewritten. Multiple empty values, pipes, tabs, newlines, backslashes and Unicode round-trip correctly. Invalid explicit counts are rejected rather than silently altering the query.
The existing SavedViews container, raw logs, game saves, statistics calculations, rulesets and limits are untouched. Existing opaque stored view strings remain preserved. Older applications still have their original inability to read a single-empty operand accurately; no downgrade compatibility for that previously broken case is claimed.

## Evidence
One baseline reproduction failed: expected one empty string, decoded zero strings. Five new permanent cases cover singleton-empty roundtrip, zero/multiple/escaped values, old records and ordinary encoding, malformed count rejection, and actual SavedViews store/load.
Compiled the actual current SavedViews production/test source with the fix. New tests plus eleven existing SavedViews tests and eighteen query-engine tests:34unique tests,0failures,0.160seconds.
Initial compile omitted an existing QuerySupport test dependency; correcting the local classpath resolved that fixture setup failure. No tests were weakened.
Full expected1244core+21API34+18PNG and debug verification are requested and pending at this source commit. TEST89 remains the last verified delivered APK. This fix is not in TEST89.

## Remaining work
Complete UIMSD audit and visual overhaul, historical gaps, simulator/custom-rules foundations, exact-owner-run bugs and physical-phone acceptance remain unfinished. Live community services and unapproved scoring/design decisions remain outside implementation authorization. No promise of uninterrupted background coding or entire-backlog completion is made.

## TEST116 verified; TEST117 candidate
# TEST117 custom health, defense, spawn and hunger candidate

TEST116 source d6c3f34594317307b949642850c24afe4e38c9d0, verification 38384131886 succeeded: downloaded XML confirms 1339 unique core and 21 API34 instrumentation cases, no failures/errors/skips, 22 valid PNGs and debug APK artifact 11805380201. It is staged, not delivered; fresh signing/application-ID/checksum publication gates and physical-phone testing are not claimed.

This package adds five gameplay effects: Enemy HP, Enemy Spawn Frequency, Hunger Depletion, Player Defense and Enemy Defense. Together with TEST116's six effects, eleven of eighteen have source implementations. Native controls are not implemented, so none is claimed alpha-complete. UIMSD work remains paused as requested; original scores are unchanged.

Enemy health applies once during initial generation and later actor insertion, with an additive optional saved flag; dead enemies remain dead and bosses/allies are excluded until the separate boss-health work. Spawn frequency affects ordinary initial density and respawn interval without changing XP or drops. Custom oversaturated placement now terminates when legal cells are exhausted; the original neutral loop is preserved. A fixture with eight legal cells and requested triple density verifies termination, not an owner-run reproduction. Hunger scales passive depletion and negative energy costs while keeping food restoration separate.

Defense scales the existing complete virtual armor/buff roll exactly once at its consumers. Direct attacks, trap/bomb reductions, warrior abilities, Skeleton/Tengu blasts and Chalice reductions use the same wrapper; immunities, resistances and shields remain separate. Chalice preview endpoints use the same defense scaling. Raw virtual overrides remain intact to avoid double scaling inherited rolls.

Nine new regression methods cover all enemy-health values, actual mob save/load and insertion, hunger values, six-class combined floor generation, bounded placement, spawn/cooldown boundaries, defense/RNG parity and Chalice preview support. A combined host run of these nine, six existing custom combat, three custom upgrade generation and eighteen existing Chalice methods passed 36 unique tests in 9.841s. Two initial host invocations failed due to missing test/dependency classpaths; corrected the harness without weakening assertions. The generation suite includes all 60 scroll totals with and without Forbidden Runes.

Full required 1348 core, 21 API34, 22 PNG and debug checks are pending for this candidate. No TEST117 APK or delivery claim. Last delivered TEST112 is retained. Seven gameplay effects remain unfinished: Player/Enemy/Boss damage, Boss HP, Food Availability, Healing Effectiveness and Equipment Availability. Damage attribution must cover all approved offensive sources, including summons and delayed effects, exactly once; it is not silently limited to ordinary attacks. No score coefficients or unrelated mechanics changed.


## TEST117 verified / TEST118 custom-rules notice candidate
# TEST118 native custom-rules window and scoring notice candidate

The owner requested the exact notice: "Custom Dungeon Rules scoring is still being developed. Difficulty settings currently do not affect your score. A scoring multiplier will be added in a future update."

No Custom Dungeon Rules window existed in TEST117's actual tree. This package adds the previously approved native window, reached from APD Settings. The exact notice is always above the scrolling controls, with existing RenderedTextBlock, OptionSlider, ScrollPane, RedButton and Window components. It never needs dismissal and does not scroll away. Save edits next-run preferences only; Back cancels draft edits. Current-run inspection is read-only and missing historical configurations remain unknown. There is no scoring or statistics-engine change.

The eleven implemented gameplay effects are editable. The seven unfinished effects are explicitly marked pending and disabled, preserving their stored values. They must be completed and enabled before the mandatory eighteen-slider alpha can be accepted. This is a partial development build, not a completed early-alpha release.

Added two host presentation regressions covering every permitted slider value/position, bounds rejection and all sixty whole-scroll percentage labels. Added an Android test that opens the actual window, touches the first slider, verifies draft cancellation, checks the fixed notice while scrolling and checks a narrow read-only layout. Two required screenshots are added to the existing runner. Android validation is pending, not presumed successful.

Damage work adds an explicit immutable origin and single-scaled-amount bridge. Boss selects Boss Damage alone; creator origin can be retained for summons without treating every ally as player-owned; delayed origin has additive metadata, missing old origin remains unattributed and invalid future metadata rejects rather than silently losing attribution. The original source object is retained for virtual target processing, immunity, resistance and death logic. Four host methods include actual engine immunity/resistance/barrier behavior, exact-once scaling, separate simultaneous origins, neutral RNG/value parity, overflow bounds and metadata restoration. This bridge is NOT yet wired through existing attacks, spells, explosions or delayed effects. None of the three damage sliders is claimed complete.

Six new host methods passed 0.216s. An exact TEST117 snapshot of 121 damage-call files contains 241 calls/definitions; this is a navigation census, not complete attribution coverage, because application-only files and merged blobs still need expansion. Poison/Burning/Corrosion/Bleeding ticks currently lose their attacker identity; effect reapplication and shared/derived damage must preserve original behavior before integration is called complete. Starvation, unattributed traps/environment and Chalice self-damage must remain unscaled.

TEST117 source6cec6e00a96302b67c1dcbbf599320375a72e4cd, run38387716215: downloaded XML confirms1348unique core and21API34, zero failures/errors/skips;22validPNG and debug assembly. APK artifact11807073393 is privately staged, not delivered/signing-published/phone-tested. TEST118 must pass1354core/22API34/24PNG/debug. Original score behavior, active-run snapshot, player data and signing/application ID are unchanged. UIMSD work remains paused as requested; recording code is not modified. Native settings do not constitute complete eighteen-slider gameplay/UIMSD/scoring acceptance.


## TEST119 exact experimental warning candidate
# TEST119 experimental custom-rules warning candidate

Replaces the earlier short scoring-only notice with the owner's exact lifetime experimental warning and separate scoring paragraph. EXPERIMENTAL FEATURE uses compact native heading styling. The full warning is first in the existing scroll area, above all controls; this avoids clipping narrow viewports or reserving so much fixed height that sliders become inaccessible. No acknowledgment, modal warning or extra action is required. The heading remains visible, and reopening starts at the warning.

Exact body:
Custom Dungeon Rules are experimental and will continue to evolve throughout APD's lifetime. Some or all difficulty sliders may have their minimum and maximum values adjusted in future updates as gameplay balance and player feedback are evaluated.

The scoring system for Custom Dungeon Rules is still being developed. These settings currently do not affect your earned score.

Lifetime warning and scoring paragraph are separate constants, so a later approved scoring implementation can update only its paragraph. No slider boundary, gameplay effect, score calculation, saved configuration, historical record or UIMSD recording code changes in this package. Previously saved rules remain untouched.

Updated the existing Android notice test for scrolling text above controls, actual slider touch/cancellation, unchanged active rules and original score, and narrow read-only rendering. Required screenshots remain custom-rules-notice.png and custom-rules-notice-narrow.png. Existing two presentation tests pass0.038s and both changed core classes compile locally. Android verification pending; do not claim these new pictures are verified until retrieved and inspected.

Previous produced APK118: source03393816054f4f3ccaa3e5fd85fae754e4f6e6e4/run38390168067. Its core/build job passed and private APK artifact11808407560 exists; its Android job was still pending when this report was prepared. It contains the older scoring-only notice, not this replacement. TEST119 requires1354core/22API34/24validPNG/debug and screenshot review. No private release/distribution or physical-phone claim.

The experimental warning does not authorize changing limits or rewriting old runs. Eleven gameplay effects are implemented through117, seven remain unfinished and disabled. The local boss-health prototype is not in this UI package. Offensive attribution groundwork is in118, but full offensive integration remains unfinished. UIMSD remains paused as requested and the eighteen-slider early alpha is not yet complete.


## TEST119 warning verified / TEST120 boss health and food candidate
# TEST120 boss health and regional food candidate

Previous produced and checked119: source217c18729f2dc909277ad8c2ce38a0d371618e37/run38391217821. Downloaded XML1354unique core and22API34, zero failed/error/skipped;24validPNG and debug assembly. The exact lifetime experimental warning was manually inspected in normal and narrow screenshots. Narrow content scrolls; this batch adds a third narrow-scrolled capture to verify access to its lower scoring paragraph. Artifact11808429916 is private/staged, not distributed or signing-published. No physical-phone evidence.

Adds Boss HP as a replacement for Enemy HP on BOSS actors. Initial/summoned health uses the existing once-only optional saved flag. Goo, Tengu, DM300 and fist thresholds already follow maximum health. Yog's fixed300/100phase boundaries and400HP beam interval now follow the selected health factor, preserving original fractions and neutral behavior. Dwarf King's fixed remaining-health and normal/stronger shield-wave thresholds also scale. Original cooldown formulas, phases, attacks and summons remain.

Compatibility failure reproduced with the old actual Dwarf/Yog classes under the proposed custom-health hook:2of3new tests fail. At25%Yog HP, its old final boundary remains100 instead of25. At25%Dwarf HP, old integerHT/12removal already leaves the wrong shield remainder by the fourth objective and would leave3shield after12normal objectives. The correction allocates the exact scaled shield across the original12or18objectives, saves the resolved count and preserves it after actual fresh-object reload. Negative or invulnerable hits do not advance the counter. Invalid stored counters reject without deleting evidence. These are custom-integration defects found before exposing Boss HP, not claimed vanilla-default bugs.

Food Availability changes only baseline main-floor food and the existing large-floor bonus. At25%, one standard ration is queued on the first ordinary floor of each region:five regional guarantees. Other allowed values distribute their base supply across four ordinary floors per region;100%keeps the original generator calls, deck state and RNG. Large-floor bonus supply follows the percentage, while shops, rooms, drops, talents, artifacts and alchemy remain untouched. Famine's existing reduced nutritional effect remains. Food is placed through normal item generation, never inserted into inventory.

Six new methods:240boss-constructor/roundtrip fixtures across20values, sixboss types and normal/stronger challenge; actualYog final boundary; actualDwarf shield progression/save reload at allvalues; regional food allocations; neutral generator/deck/RNG parity;60real first-region-floor fixtures acrosssixclasses and normal/Famine. Combined48unique host methods pass14.153s. Initial food fixture failures were due to not restoring the generator deck for its reference and not counting normal mimic-held loot; the corrected assertions retain the ration requirement. An initial boss fixture incorrectly restored buffs into an existing object; changed to the actual fresh-object load path. No tests disabled or deleted. The combined high-spawn/high-health/fast-hunger/low-upgrade fixture now includes25%food.

Thirteen gameplay effects have source implementations. Five remain incomplete/disabled:Player Damage, Enemy Damage, Boss Damage, Healing Effectiveness and Equipment Availability. Full offensive attribution is still pending; its source-preserving bridge is groundwork, not a completed damage slider. Full required1360core/22API34/25PNG/debug and new screenshot review are pending. UIMSD remains paused until gameplay integration is complete; its permanent acceptance requirements are not claimed complete. Original scoring, provisional bounds, lifetime warning, player data, signing/applicationID are preserved. No automatic distribution.


# Delayed/overlapping attack audio — lower-priority unconfirmed upstream report

Owner evidence, corrected scope:
- Observed in vanilla Shattered Pixel Dungeon on RedMagic11Pro, Android16, built-in speakers; no headphones.
- Several attack sounds sometimes appear absent, then play together after a delay.
- Most prominently noticed in Prison and during Tengu. These are observations, NOT boundaries; other regions were not closely observed or are not remembered.
- Other games have not shown this symptom to the owner. This does not establish a device or game root cause.
- APD is not confirmed affected; approximately one APD run has been played.
- Whether gameplay/frame rate stalls simultaneously is unknown.

Status: source/audio investigation and audible reproduction pending; no root cause, inherited-defect proof or fix claimed. Current API34 UI/core checks are not acoustic RedMagic/Android16 reproduction evidence.

Later compare actual upstream/APD sample playback, sound loading, callback/actor/render scheduling and Android lifecycle. Include Prison enemies and Tengu attacks, teleports, traps and bombs without assuming region/Tengu causation. Distinguish late triggers from on-time requests with late audible playback. Correlate frame stalls, GC and contention only with measurements. Preserve combat/damage/timing, intended simultaneous effects and the existing audio system. Do not suppress sounds merely to hide symptoms; stale-event discarding requires evidence. Keep open if unconfirmed.

Priority: below October16early-alpha sliders, save/progression stability, scoring interfaces and mandatory UIMSD configuration recording. No audio changes inTEST120.

## 2026-10-16T03:38:42Z — active-play idle-gap correction candidate
- Audited `PlayTracker` against its stated active-time contract and found that every app-background or long-pause gap over 30 seconds was credited as exactly 30 seconds of active play because the accumulator used `min(gap, 30 seconds)`.
- Added retained method `aLongIdleGapAddsNoActivePlayTime`: it moves the monotonic baseline 31 seconds back, records the next hero turn and requires the active total to remain zero. Exact verified TEST232 source `96772420c7ccdcbc294e8eae33d68b27d71abfaa` must fail with 30,000 milliseconds.
